CSV row counts match logs (18,174 ctrl / 18,354 varA). BQ_CACHE_THREADS=1 set per registry note 6. I re-ran compare_dcf.py and loo_dsr_dcf.py: every quoted number matched to the stated precision. Canonical wtnamecap.csv untouched (mtime 2026-07-12), §8 OK. ctrl (27.09) != registry-pinned R3 (27.84) but this is the data/bq_cache vintage the runner comment documents; the ctrl/varA pair is contemporaneous, so the paired delta is valid.", "param_overfit": "fail — MoS>0 and robust=all-4-sensitivity-box are single points with no sensitivity grid (no plateau demonstrated); B's W=0.25 is likewise a single point. Aggravating: 2 variants tested, 1 passed — a 50% hit rate on a gate whose underlying delta has p=0.31 is exactly what chance produces.", "capacity_adv_realism": "pass — DCF overlay touches only the custom30V parking basket; median ADV 1,671.0B/day at 20% cap -> ~334B/day parkable vs 50B NAV. No micro-name dependence.", "arithmetic_mechanism": "fail — arithmetic reconciles exactly (I reproduced IS/OOS/FULL and the per-year table), but the stated MECHANISM is wrong. Finding asserts 'edge Sharpe DUONG o CA 14/14 -> edge Sharpe BEN, khong do 1-2 nam ganh'. The 14/14 is a near-tautology: each LOO fold shares ~92% of its observations with the full sample, so a positive full-sample delta with no dominant single year forces all folds positive. Direct decomposition shows the return edge is 100.2% from 2017+2021. Additionally DSR is not merely 'weak': var_SR=4.35e-06 computed across three ~98%-correlated variants gives SR0=0.0018 vs system SR/obs=0.1134, so DSR tests 'is V2.4 Sharpe > ~0' and returns 1.0 for any variant whatsoever.", "_extra_significance": "fail — paired t-test on daily log-return delta (varA - ctrl), n=3,106: mean +3.122e-05/day, t=1.02, p=0.306. Daily corr(A,ctrl)=0.981."}, "independent_recompute": "Re-ran compare_dcf.py and loo_dsr_dcf.py from the cited CSVs — ALL headline numbers matched exactly (ctrl IS 23.37/1.54/-18.0/1.30, A IS 23.87/1.59/-16.2/1.48, A OOS 32.08/1.97/-18.8/1.71, FULL 28.08/1.79; LOO 13/14 with the sole FAIL on drop-2017 dCalmar -0.010; DSR 1.0000 at N=3 and N=8). Independently verified ticker_financial.time == Release_Date via duckdb (98.82% exact, 1.16% <=11d early) confirming the PIT guard. Then ran two NEW tests the finding did not: (1) paired t-test on the daily delta -> t=1.02, p=0.306; (2) joint drop of BOTH carrier years -> dSharpe +0.059 -> +0.015, and IS ex-2017 -> -0.006 (gate flips to FAIL). Reproduction of the finding's own arithmetic is perfect; the inference does not survive.", "killer_objection": "The pre-registered gate passes on a delta with t=1.02 / p=0.31, and 100.2% of the cumulative return edge comes from 2017 (+5.51pp) and 2021 (+4.21pp) — one year inside each gate window. Remove 2017 and the IS Sharpe delta goes NEGATIVE; the 'PASS both windows' result is one lucky year per window, not two independent confirmations. The '14/14 positive Sharpe' that motivated the broad-based read is a mathematical artifact of 92% data overlap between LOO folds, not evidence of breadth. Mechanistically this is ~3 names swapped out of a 30-name PARKING basket; there is no placebo showing that a random 3-name swap wouldn't produce the same +/-1pp spread.", "recommended_reruns": ["PLACEBO (decisive, blocking): drop 2-3 RANDOM names per rebalance from the custom30V pool, 20+ seeds, same harness/cache/AUDIT_END. If the placebo CAGR/Sharpe delta distribution brackets variant A's (+0.99pp / +0.059), DCF is doing no work and the direction should be closed.", "Report the delta t-stat / p-value and a block-bootstrap CI (e.g. 250-day blocks, 10k resamples) on the A-minus-ctrl return series as a MANDATORY field on any future selector-overlay finding — the gate alone cannot distinguish +1pp of signal from +1pp of noise.", "Fix loo_dsr_dcf.py::dsr — compute DSR on the INCREMENT (A-minus-ctrl return series) or set var_SR from the true trial universe (every DCF config tried across Pha 1-3, incl. Study A/B), not the variance across 3 correlated survivors. As written it returns 1.0 unconditionally and must not be cited as evidence.", "Replace LOO-by-year with block-bootstrap or drop-K (K>=3) resampling; LOO on 13 years cannot falsify a full-sample sign given ~92% fold overlap.", "If the direction is kept alive after a passing placebo: sensitivity grid on the MoS threshold (-5%/0/+5%) and on the robust definition (all-4-box vs 3-of-4 vs none) to show a plateau rather than a knife-edge at MoS>0.", "Explain/close the ctrl-vs-canonical gap (27.09 vs pinned 27.84) by re-syncing data/bq_cache to the AUDIT_END=2026-06-19 vintage, so future experiments pair against a control that reproduces the registry."], "summary": "The finding's plumbing is clean and its arithmetic is exactly reproducible — the three runs really did finish (exit=0, self-check 0 VND, canonical CSV untouched), the DCF overlay is genuinely look-ahead-free (fair_value gates on fin.time <= asof, and I verified ticker_financial.time IS the release date), capacity is a non-issue, and every quoted metric re-ran to the digit. What does not survive is the inference. The A-minus-ctrl daily return delta has t=1.02 / p=0.31 over 3,106 sessions: the +0.99pp CAGR is noise. Worse, the one thing the finding asserted positively — that the Sharpe edge is broad-based and 'not carried by 1-2 years' — is false: 2017 (+5.51pp) plus 2021 (+4.21pp) account for 100.2% of the total cumulative delta, dropping both cuts dSharpe from +0.059 to +0.015, and removing 2017 alone flips the IS Sharpe delta negative, i.e. the pre-registered gate fails. The '14/14 positive' LOO result is an artifact of ~92% fold overlap, not independent confirmation, and DSR=1.0 is not merely 'weak' — with var_SR computed across three 98%-correlated variants it reduces to 'is V2.4's Sharpe above zero' and would pass anything. The gate passed because one lucky year landed in each window. The finding was right to withhold GO and right to flag the DSR and Calmar caveats; it was wrong on the Sharpe-breadth claim, which is the claim the case rests on. Do not wire. The blocking experiment is a random-name-swap placebo on the parking basket: this overlay changes ~3 names out of 30 in a PARKING vehicle, and until a placebo distribution is shown not to bracket +/-1pp, there is no evidence DCF rather than chance selected 2017's and 2021's winners."}

IS 2014-19 z=0.68 (5/20, NOT an outlier); OOS 2020+ z=2.85 (0/20). Edge does not collapse post-2020; it is OOS-concentrated, the opposite of the classic IS-overfit signature. Finding states this itself ('IS KHONG outlier').", "panel_curation_bias": "pass — universe is a BQ-live liquidity gate, pool_min=pool_max=60 on all 48 rebal dates (data/dcf_exp_logs/placebo_drops_seed*.csv), identical across ctrl/varA/placebo. No hardcoded names, no CAPIT-style curation. Claim is a delta (+0.99pp vs ctrl), not a >30%-CAGR level claim.", "reproducibility_selfcheck": "pass — re-ran dcf_placebo_test.py under /home/trido/thanhdt/wc_venv/bin/python: ALL headline numbers match to the quoted digit (FULL dCAGR +0.993 vs null -0.088+/-0.430 z=2.51 0/20; FULL dSharpe +0.059 vs +0.012+/-0.021 z=2.26 0/20; OOS dCAGR +1.497 z=2.85 0/20; OOS dSharpe +0.063 z=2.53 0/20; IS dCAGR +0.493 z=0.68 5/20; t_as_draw/p_as_draw all match). Regression guard independently verified by md5: ctrl and ctrl-rerun both cc8988bc4b078e18359bc6c9c9984acf -> byte-identical, patch provably did not move the OFF path. 20/20 seed logs show 'cash-flow identity max err = 0 VND; final NAV identity err = 0 VND' on BOTH books (BAL+LAG). Drop logs: 740 drops/48 dates/mean 15.42 on every seed as claimed.", "param_overfit": "pass — the placebo control has no tuned parameters; n_d is measured off the same dcf_at calls, not chosen. varA's gate (MoS>0, robust across the +/-1pp-r/+/-2pp-g box) has no fitted threshold. One real resolution limit: with n=20 seeds a 0/20 result bottoms out at p~1/21~0.048, and the 20-draw SD estimate is itself ~16% noisy, so z=2.51 carries roughly a [2.1,3.1] CI. The finding flags this correctly ('descriptive, khong phai p-value de bao ve').", "capacity_adv_realism": "na — this is a delta test in which ctrl, varA and all 20 placebo seeds share an identical liquidity gate (top-60 pool) and identical NAV_TOTAL_B=50 / ETF_LIQ=custompitg / BASKET_WT=namecap config. Capacity is held constant by construction and cannot drive the spread.", "arithmetic_mechanism": "fail (non-fatal, and conservative in direction) — the finding's fairness_audit claims '~3 trong so do cham toi ro 30 ten cuoi' and that the placebo differs from varA 'only in WHICH names go'. Measured directly off the CUSTOM_MEMBERS rows: varA changes 5.19 names/rebal vs ctrl, the placebo changes 7.72 (range 7.29-8.21) = a 1.49x perturbation mismatch. Counts are matched at the POOL level, not at the BASKET level where the effect lives, so the stated '~3' is a 73% understatement and the 'only difference is which names go' framing is inaccurate. Direction check: a harder-perturbing placebo yields a WIDER null (SD 0.43 vs a matched ~0.35), which makes varA's outlier status harder to reach, not easier — a matched null would push z toward ~3.0. The mislabeling is therefore conservative and does not void the headline. Null mean is only -0.09, so the selector-adversarial handicap of random dropping explains <10% of the spread. Per-year, IS and FULL figures otherwise reconcile (2017 +7.52 + 2021 +8.71 ~ the full +16.2pp/13yr).", "_loo_year_attack": "the decisive new check — see independent_recompute / killer_objection"}, "independent_recompute": "Re-ran dcf_placebo_test.py end-to-end: every quoted number reproduced to the quoted precision (FULL dCAGR z=2.51 0/20, OOS z=2.85 0/20, IS z=0.68 5/20, per-year 2017 +7.52 / 2021 +8.71). Independently md5-verified the regression guard (ctrl == ctrl-rerun, byte-identical). Independently verified the PIT premise against the parquet (time == Release_Date, 58264/66387). Independently measured basket perturbation off CUSTOM_MEMBERS: varA 5.19 names vs placebo 7.72 (contradicts the finding's '~3'). Ran a NEW leave-one-year-out on the null that the finding did not run: excluding 2021 collapses varA to z=1.35 / 2-20; excluding 2017+2021 gives varA delta +0.01pp, z=0.74, 5/20.", "killer_objection": "The 2.5-SD outlier is a one-year artifact. My leave-one-year-out on the null shows that removing 2021 alone drops varA from z=2.58 (0/20) to z=1.35 (2/20) — an ordinary draw from the null — and removing 2017+2021 leaves varA at +0.01pp (z=0.74, 5/20), i.e. literally indistinguishable from a random swap. The finding acknowledges the 2017/2021 concentration qualitatively but does not disclose that the very statistic it is reporting (the FULL-window outlier) fails to survive deletion of a single year. So while 'random swap does not reproduce varA' is correctly computed and true as stated, it is not evidence of a durable, repeatable DCF mechanism — it is evidence that one year (2021) was unusually kind to the specific names the DCF excluded.", "recommended_reruns": ["Value-proxy placebo (the finding's own next step, and the right one): at each rebal drop the SAME n_d names by highest PE (or lowest 1/PE+1/PCF rank) instead of by DCF. If that reproduces varA's spread, the DCF machinery adds nothing over a trivial value screen and the chain is dead. This must run BEFORE any GO discussion.", "Perturbation-MATCHED null: re-run the placebo constrained to change 5.19 names/rebal in the final basket (match at the CUSTOM_MEMBERS level, not the pool level), so the null answers the skeptic's actual objection. Expect z to rise (~3.0), so this strengthens rather than threatens the result — but it makes the control honest and retires the incorrect '~3 names' claim.", "Judge every future DCF variant on the leave-one-year-out basis, not the full-window z. Report z_LOO(2021) and z_LOO(2017,2021) alongside any headline; a gate whose edge dies on one-year deletion is not deployable regardless of full-window z.", "Restrict the random draw to the DCF-COMPUTABLE subset. varA can structurally never drop a NOT_COMPUTED name, but the placebo can — so the placebo draws from a superset, which is a second (smaller) reason the two are not the same experiment.", "Raise seeds from 20 to >=200 for any null that will support a GO. At n=20 a 0/20 result cannot distinguish p=0.05 from p=0.001, and the SD driving z is ~16% noisy.", "Correct the two factual errors in dcf_valuation_framework.md s7.3 + data/results_registry.md: the '~3 names touch the final basket' figure (measured 5.19) and the 'ONLY difference is which names go' framing (perturbation is 1.49x larger)."], "summary": "The finding reproduces exactly and its artifacts are unusually clean: I re-ran dcf_placebo_test.py and every quoted number matched to the digit, the regression guard is genuinely md5-identical (proving the placebo branch left the production OFF path untouched, so production impact really is ZERO), all 20 seeds self-check to 0 VND on both books, and the DCF's point-in-time premise holds under independent inspection (ticker_financial.time really is the release date, 58264/66387 exact). I found two errors, both non-fatal: the placebo is matched to varA at the pool level but not the basket level (varA changes 5.19 names/rebal, the placebo 7.72 — a 1.49x mismatch, contradicting the finding's '~3' and its 'only which names go' framing), and this makes the null wider than it should be, which is conservative — a matched null would push varA's z toward 3.0, not below 2. So the headline as literally stated survives: a same-sized random swap does not reproduce varA's +0.99pp. The real damage is an attack the finding did not run. On leave-one-year-out, deleting 2021 alone collapses varA from z=2.58/0-of-20 to z=1.35/2-of-20, and deleting 2017+2021 leaves it at +0.01pp (z=0.74, 5/20) — the outlier is entirely a one-year artifact. The finding flags the 2017/2021 concentration in prose but never shows that its own headline statistic dies on single-year deletion. Verdict is CONFIRMED because the finding's claims are narrow, correctly computed, reproducible, and it explicitly refuses to self-declare GO while routing here — which was the right call. But this is much weaker evidence of a durable DCF mechanism than z=2.51 implies, and the value-proxy placebo plus a LOO-year criterion must both clear before GO is even discussable."}

The real capital weight is roughly double (44.4% BANK-only, 47.4% financial full-period, 82.7% in 2026Q2), which means the 30% cap that was advised as a loose ceiling is really a cut-half-the-exposure intervention — the finding's core point, and it is right. I recomputed all four arms from the raw audit CSVs rather than the logs and every quoted figure matched exactly; self-checks are 0 VND on both BAL and LAG, borrow is 0, gross is 1.000, EXP_TAG is on every arm including the baseline, and threads=1. The metric fix is genuinely return-neutral (A2-A0 = -0.05pp with an IS/OOS sign flip = noise), the 30% cap is genuinely NO-GO (-0.64pp negative in BOTH windows with MaxDD frozen at -17.6), and the sectorcap bug the job caught is real in the code. I found three defects, and all of them argue for MORE caution rather than less: the DY hit-rate binomial suffers the same overlapping-window problem the finding correctly diagnosed in its own t-stat, and correcting it drops the confound-controlled cut from p=0.011 to p=0.067, so 'supported at 6M' overstates; A3-vs-A2 is not the clean one-axis contrast claimed, because fincap preserves the group budget while namecap leaks mega-cap excess out of it (I reproduced this — in 5 quarters the 'cap' actually RAISES financial weight); and 'binding 100% of days from 2017Q1' is really 86.8% of quarters. None of these void a verdict whose entire recommendation is to touch nothing. The verdict rests on a structural argument rather than noisy deltas, and I verified that structure against live BigQuery rather than their cached artifacts: the largest golden basket in 12 years is 8 names against a nsmallest(15) cap, so the dividend criterion can only cut and never select, and 79/84 name-events already pay dividends because the ROE/ROIC/FSCORE quality gate implies it. The mechanism makes a falsifiable prediction that holds — zero gate bites in IS forces A1 IS to equal A0 IS exactly, and it does (23.09%, +0.00) — with the entire -0.32pp Full traceable to 4 name removals at 2 OOS events, which the finding itself correctly refuses to treat as evidence of systematic harm. My strongest attack was that dy3 is mechanically a cheapness ratio and high-dividend names are low-beta defensives, so the drawdown edge could be an uncontrolled beta effect (exactly how the custom30V claim died the same morning); I pulled Beta and re-ran their regression, and the effect strengthened to +3.11pp with beta insignificant. Leave-one-episode-out is robust across every fold. The one real defect is inferential inflation: the n=8 bootstrap p-values (0.002/0.001) should be 0.035/0.074, meaning the 6M result is not significant at 5% — a caveat that changes no action here since nothing is wired, but which should be corrected in the registry and must not be leaned on if the dividend idea is ever re-scoped. Production impact is zero and the default-off path is byte-identical."}
